#### v3.X-next.6 follow-up (2026-05-22; operator-recovery affordances on `DynamicTraceRunControl`)

Small post-v3.X-next.2 follow-up under DEC-031's umbrella. v3.X-next.2's `Stop` button deliberately preserves runtime overlays (`firedMethods` / `liveValues` / `summaries` / `hookFailed`) for post-mortem inspection — operator stops the Frida session and continues to see what fired. That posture left no in-UI recovery path for "I mis-clicked in the Android app and want to redo the trace": pre-v3.X-next.6 the only options were anchor-switch (heavy-handed — fires the `anchorKey` reset useEffect that also clears Inspector selection), app-switch (heavier), or full page reload. Operator surfaced the gap 2026-05-21 during dogfood ("Is there a way to reset if during a dynamic trace I hit the wrong UI interfaces in the android app …").

**Policy ratified at the v3.X-next.6 design Ask:** **(c) Both buttons** — Restart (primary recovery; visible whenever there's runtime state OR an active session) + Clear overlay (tertiary; visible only when there's runtime state AND the session is not running). Rejected alternatives: (a) strict / clear-only (operator must manually `Stop → Clear → Run` for the "I mis-clicked" workflow — too many keystrokes for the dominant use case); (b) restart-only (denies the "I'm done inspecting, give me a blank overlay" workflow that's distinct from "redo the trace"). Operator picked (c) explicitly via the three-option Ask.

**Implementation locked:** **Restart** = `if (running) await stop(); void start(prevReq)` (the hook's `start()` already wipes state to `INITIAL_DYNAMIC_TRACE_STATE` before re-arming so no explicit `reset()` is needed between stop + start — observation that simplified the handler). **Clear overlay** = `reset()` only (pure local-state wipe; doesn't touch the BE — the BE's session-timeout GC catches any abandoned session, matching the `useDynamicTrace` unmount cleanup posture from Phase 13 v1 / 13.8). Visibility-flag computation: `hasRuntimeState = firedMethods.size > 0 || liveValues.size > 0 || summaries.size > 0 || hookFailed.size > 0`; `showRestart = (isRunning || hasRuntimeState) && !isBusy`; `showClear = hasRuntimeState && !isRunning && !isBusy`. Clear is hidden during `running` because clearing while events stream in would just re-populate immediately (misleading affordance).

**Files (2 code + 3 docs):** `androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/LabTraceMode.tsx` (~+45 LOC; `DynamicTraceRunControl` destructure + 2 callbacks + 3 visibility flags + 2 conditional `<button>` elements + doc-comment block); `androscan/web/frontend/src/App.css` (~+25 LOC; `.lab-dynamic-trace-button-restart` warn-on-hover + `.lab-dynamic-trace-button-clear` muted-default-text-on-hover); `docs/TASKS.md` (Active Task block + v3.X-next.6 row marked `[x]`); `docs/STATE.md` (Recent completed work entry + Next expected milestone flipped); this DECISIONS.md follow-up note.

**Verification:** pytest 1330 → 1330 (zero BE touched); vitest 29/29 (no new tests — pure UI affordance, no logic surface); `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean (CSS +0.35 KB raw / JS +0.90 KB raw — net trivial). No new dependencies, no schema changes, no new tests, no new architectural locks. **DEC-031 status:** stays Active. v3.X-next.6 is the second small follow-up under DEC-031's umbrella (the first being the v3.X-next.5 candidate stub still in the backlog for shimmer polish) — neither introduces a new decision-tier lock.

#### v3.X-next.3.0 follow-up (2026-05-22; planning checkpoint for v3.X-next.3 invoke-gap recovery via shared `call_graph` resolver path)

Docs-only ratification commit for the v3.X-next.3 implementation beat — mirrors the v3.X-next.1.0 → v3.X-next.1 + v3.X-next.2.0 → v3.X-next.2 docs-then-code cadence per Q1=(b′) split-under-path-B. The original v3.X-next.3 candidate stub (added at v3.X-next.2.0 close) framed the work as "Q5=(b)/(c) `method_invocations` gap-recovery via `call_graph.sqlite::neighbors()`" gated on dogfood evidence of orphan-leaf rendering. Plan-mode pre-read of [`androscan/analysis/slicing.py:1668-1683`](androscan/analysis/slicing.py) refined the diagnosis into **two distinct invoke-gap scenarios** with very different visual risk, and dissolved a third "framework-inherited noise" hypothesis as a phantom problem — so v3.X-next.3.0 lands a sharper Q-lock matrix than the v3.X-next.2.0 stub had.

**Refined diagnosis (load-bearing for Q1).** Today's `extract_call_sites` drops invoke edges in exactly two scenarios — both silently, both invisible to operators unless instrumented: **(A) cross-module unresolvable** — `owner not in classes_by_smali` AND no `call_graph_resolver` wired → `continue` at line 1683; **(B) inherited-from-app-superclass** — `owner in classes_by_smali` but the specific `target_sig` isn't a method body declared on that class (it's inherited from an app-code parent); today the slicer happily emits a CallSite pointing at a non-existent body and the FE renders a phantom node that looks like a terminal even though it should keep walking. **Crucially:** when a CallSite IS built (either branch), the `instruction_index` comes from the slicer's local `enumerate(method_decisions.instructions)` loop and `in_branch_of` / `branch_label` come from `dominance.get(idx)` — both computed locally per the v3.X-next.1 lockset — so a resolver-recovered CallSite carries **full CFG metadata, byte-equal-shape to a same-module one**. The FE emitter at [`androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/executionFlowGraphV3.ts`](androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/executionFlowGraphV3.ts) consumes them through the same `emitInvokeEdges` path with zero special handling. **Visual risk: LOW** — the existing trace can only GAIN edges, never lose or restyle any, and the v3.X-next.2 vitest suite (29/29) pins the emitter's shape contract on the same data envelope these recovered CallSites carry.

**Phantom hypothesis explicitly rejected.** "Framework-inherited method noise" — `MainActivity.getResources()` inherited from `Landroid/app/Activity;` — is NOT a real gap. The smali assembler resolves invoke-virtual to the *declaring* class at compile time per Dalvik bytecode convention, so the smali `invoke-*` line carries `Landroid/app/Activity;->getResources()` directly + the `FRAMEWORK_CLASS_PREFIXES` filter at `slicing.py:1660` catches it before any resolution work runs. Pivoting v3.X-next.3 to "drop inherited-framework-noise" would have been chasing a non-existent bug.

**Original Q5=(b)/(c) `synthesise-from-neighbors()` framing also explicitly rejected** (as the still-recorded diminishing-returns analysis at v3.X-next.2.0 already established). Synthesised orphan-leaf CallSites would lack `instruction_index` + dominance entirely (those are properties of a calling-method's instruction stream, not properties of the call graph's adjacency), so they would render as the flat horizontal fan DEC-031's diagnosis was meant to fix. High visual risk + low dogfood-empirical value (most WeakBank "gap-case" methods are correct terminals — getters with `const-string` + `return-object` and nothing else to recover).

**Planning-checkpoint Q-locks (5 implementation-detail Qs locked verbatim at v3.X-next.3.0; zero operator divergence — operator picked scope `(e)` (combined (a) inherited-from-app-superclass + (b) cross-module via shared resolver) at the plan-mode 2-question AskQuestion + commit-shape `(i)` two-commit split):**

| # | Question | Answer | Rationale |
|---|----------|--------|-----------|
| Q1 | Visual-safety scope — pick (b) cross-module resolver only / (a) inherited-framework-noise (phantom — rejected) / (e) combined (a) inherited-from-app-superclass + (b) cross-module via shared resolver / (d) synthesise from `neighbors()` (high-risk — rejected) / (c) defer entirely | **(e) Combined (a) inherited-from-app-superclass + (b) cross-module via shared resolver** | Operator pick at the plan-mode 2-question Ask. Recovered CallSites share the same shape regardless of which gap class they fall into, so unifying them under one resolver path is cheaper than two separate hooks. The framework-inherited phantom is explicitly rejected as a non-bug. The `synthesise-from-neighbors()` framing stays rejected per the v3.X-next.2.0 diminishing-returns analysis (carried forward verbatim). Defer-entirely is rejected because the v3.X-next.1 `extract_call_sites` `call_graph_resolver` hook stays dead code indefinitely otherwise — wire-up is small enough to be worth doing now. |
| Q2 | Resolver public-API shape | **(a) Thin new public `call_graph.lookup_method_ref(decompile_cache_dir, smali_id) -> Optional[MethodRef]`** wrapping the existing private `_lookup_node` with per-call connection; on hit parse `smali_id` into `MethodRef`; returns `None` on miss or `sqlite3.Error` | Per-call connection is correct because (i) the call is rare (only fires on cross-module / inherited targets, not the common same-module direct path) and (ii) memoization lives one layer up in `trace_behavior` where the lifetime is bounded to a single anchor build. Keeping the wrapper stateless mirrors the `neighbors()` / `paths()` public-API shape already established in `call_graph.py` (each opens its own `_connect()` context). Rejected: (b) reuse `neighbors()` as the lookup vehicle (returns rich per-node payload we don't need + pays for caller/callee enumeration when we just want the central node); (c) expose `_lookup_node` directly (breaks the private-helper boundary). |
| Q3 | Resolver memoization shape | **(a) Per-trace dict-memoization in a closure captured by `_walk_closure`; lifetime = one anchor build** | The same `target_sig` recurs across multiple caller bodies in any non-trivial closure (e.g. `Log.d` would call out 50× if it weren't framework-filtered; legitimately reused app-code helpers easily hit 5-10×). Per-anchor memoization gives us the cache lifetime we want without complicating `call_graph.lookup_method_ref`'s API. Rejected: (b) module-level memoization in `call_graph.py` (lifetime would span the entire process, complicating cache invalidation if the call_graph rebuilds mid-process; also a hidden global that's hard to unit-test); (c) no memoization (wastes SQLite connections on hot-path duplicates). |
| Q4 | Resolver stats observability | **(a) INFO-log resolver stats on `trace_behavior` completion: `{hits_inherited, hits_cross_module, misses, total}`** — no `BehaviorAnchor` payload changes, no FE surface area | Operator can `grep` the log after re-tracing a real app to confirm whether the (a)/(b) paths actually fired. If both hit-counts are zero on WeakBank, the wire-up is harmless dead code; evidence still carries forward to the next dogfood subject. No need to bloat `BehaviorAnchor` with diagnostic counters that have zero rendering value. Rejected: (b) `BehaviorAnchor` payload field for FE display (over-engineering for a developer-instrumentation signal); (c) both (premature surface-area expansion); (d) silent (loses the empirical-evidence lever for future v3.X-next.3 follow-ups). |
| Q5 | Slicer-side branch unification for inherited-vs-cross-module | **(a) Same-module branch in `extract_call_sites` checks `target_sig in cls.method_sigs`; on miss, defer to resolver (treats inherited-from-app-superclass identically to cross-module). Framework filter at line 1660 stays untouched as the outer gate** | Unifies (a) and (b) into one resolver path; `cls.method_sigs` is a frozenset computed once per `cls` at function entry for O(1) per-invoke cost. Framework filter wins first as today so the resolver never sees framework targets (which are correctly resolved at the invoke-line by the smali assembler). Rejected: (b) two separate resolver callables, one per gap type (gratuitous interface complexity; stats split happens cleanly in `trace_behavior` regardless); (c) walk the superclass chain in the slicer (over-couples the slicer to `ClassDecl.superclass` traversal — `call_graph.sqlite` already indexes the *declaring* class for inherited methods, so the resolver gets us there for free). |

**Implementation-detail Q to settle at code time** (not a Q-lock; surfaces during commit 2): the resolver closure in `trace_behavior._walk_closure` needs to distinguish `hits_inherited` from `hits_cross_module` for the Q4 stats. Two equally-cheap shapes: (i) one callable that accepts a `context: bool` flag from the slicer (`extract_call_sites` passes True when in the same-module branch + False when in the cross-module branch); (ii) two separate callables threaded through (`call_graph_resolver_inherited` + `call_graph_resolver_cross_module`). Q5=(a)'s lock says the slicer treats both cases identically inside the body of `extract_call_sites`; the stat-split is a `trace_behavior`-side concern. Either shape preserves the Q-lock; the choice is local-cosmetic and gets locked at code-time.

**Commit shape:** operator picked **(i) two-commit split** at the plan-mode 2-question Ask — this docs-only v3.X-next.3.0 ratification commit first, then the v3.X-next.3 implementation. Mirrors the v3.X-next.1.0 → v3.X-next.1 + v3.X-next.2.0 → v3.X-next.2 cadence per DEC-031 Q1=(b′). Rejected: (ii) single combined commit (loses the planning-record-committed-before-code discipline that the v3.X-next track has held to since v3.X-next.1.0).

**Visual-safety guarantees (carried into the v3.X-next.3 implementation contract):**
- **Zero FE changes.** Recovered CallSites carry full CFG metadata + flow through the existing `emitInvokeEdges` path with byte-equal data shape.
- **vitest 29/29 should pass unchanged.** The existing emitter tests pin shape on linear / branchy / nested-locals — recovered CallSites match that shape exactly.
- **Edges can only appear, never disappear or restyle.** Worst-case visual regression on a fresh re-trace = a new edge surfaces where there wasn't one before. That's the intended improvement.
- **Cached traces are unaffected until re-traced.** Existing WeakBank flowchart won't move until the operator re-traces.

**Files touched (3 docs):** `docs/TASKS.md` (new Active Task block + new v3.X-next.3.0 backlog row + v3.X-next.3 row updated with locked Q1-Q5 + Phase 13 meta-row updated), `docs/STATE.md` (Recent completed work entry + Next expected milestone flipped to "v3.X-next.3 — implementation beat"), `docs/DECISIONS.md` (this follow-up note appended under DEC-031 closing chain). **No code touched anywhere.** **Verification:** docs-only sweep; pytest unchanged at **1330 passed**; vitest unchanged at **29/29 passed**; `tsc --noEmit` clean from v3.X-next.6; `vite build` unchanged; zero lint impact.

**DEC-031 status:** stays Active. v3.X-next.3.0 is the third small follow-up under DEC-031's umbrella (after v3.X-next.6 + the v3.X-next.5 candidate stub) — neither v3.X-next.3.0 nor the v3.X-next.3 implementation introduces a new decision-tier lock (the Q1-Q5 are implementation-detail). The Q3=(c) hybrid `call_graph_resolver` hook architected in v3.X-next.1's `extract_call_sites` finally gets wired at v3.X-next.3 — closing the "future-extension point" status it has carried since v3.X-next.1's close. **Next slot: v3.X-next.3 — implementation beat** (backend code + tests; ~50 LOC new + ~7 new tests + zero FE changes; trigger for promotion: automatic on v3.X-next.3.0 close per the committed-follow-up cadence — mirror of v3.X-next.1.0 → v3.X-next.1 + v3.X-next.2.0 → v3.X-next.2).

#### v3.X-next.3 closing note (2026-05-22; invoke-gap recovery via shared `call_graph` resolver path landed)

Implementation beat landed on the same calendar day as the v3.X-next.3.0 ratification, mirroring the v3.X-next.1.0 → v3.X-next.1 + v3.X-next.2.0 → v3.X-next.2 docs-then-code pairs per DEC-031 Q1=(b′) split-under-path-B. **All five locked implementation-detail Q&As translated 1-1 to production code** with one implementation-detail Q resolved at code time (not a Q-lock — surfaced in the v3.X-next.3.0 plan's "settle at code time" note + closed below).

**Implementation-detail Q resolved at code time:** the resolver closure in `trace_behavior._walk_closure` distinguishes `hits_inherited` from `hits_cross_module` for the Q4 stats by **parsing the owner descriptor off `target_sig` directly** (`target_sig.find(";->")` + slice — cheap string split that mirrors `_RE_INVOKE`'s owner capture group at the slicer's line 1658) and checking `owner_desc in classes_by_smali`. Shape (i) "one callable with context flag from slicer" was rejected at code time because it would have changed the existing `call_graph_resolver` callable signature from `Callable[[str], Optional[MethodRef]]` to `Callable[[str, bool], Optional[MethodRef]]`, breaking the 3 existing `test_resolver_*` tests + complicating the slicer-side contract. Owner-parse-from-target_sig keeps the resolver signature 1:1 with v3.X-next.1's Q3=(c) hook + delivers the same stat-split as either of the planned shapes — strictly better than both.

**Code landed:**
- [`androscan/analysis/call_graph.py`](androscan/analysis/call_graph.py) — new public `lookup_method_ref(decompile_cache_dir, smali_id) -> Optional[MethodRef]` (+~85 LOC: +1 import + 1 function with ~75-LOC docstring + body). Opens read-only connection via existing `_connect()`, calls private `_lookup_node` (UNIQUE-indexed on `smali_id`), on hit parses row's `smali_id` back into `MethodRef` via `MethodRef.from_smali_signature`. Returns `None` on miss / empty input / missing DB file / malformed-smali_id-in-row / any `sqlite3.Error`. Per-call connection per Q2=(a); stateless wrapper mirrors `neighbors()`/`paths()` public-API shape.
- [`androscan/analysis/slicing.py`](androscan/analysis/slicing.py) — `extract_call_sites` extended with Q5=(a) presence check (+~30 LOC net). New `method_sigs_by_class: dict[str, Optional[frozenset[str]]]` pre-computed at function entry; same-module branch checks `target_sig in cls.method_sigs` and defers to resolver on miss (unifies inherited-from-app-superclass with cross-module under one resolver path per Q5=(a)). Sentinel-tolerance preserved (None-valued classes_by_smali entries → "trust the owner check, assume method is present" — preserves byte-equal behaviour for the 30+ existing V3XNext1 tests using `_app_classes()` sentinel mode). Docstring extended ~25 LOC recording the Q5=(a) extension + the framework-inherited phantom rejection + the sentinel-tolerance contract.
- [`androscan/skills/trace_behavior.py`](androscan/skills/trace_behavior.py) — closure-level resolver closure + stats dict + INFO-log emit (+~80 LOC including ~50 LOC inline doc-comments). `resolver_cache: dict[str, Optional[MethodRef]]` + `resolver_stats = {hits_inherited:0, hits_cross_module:0, misses:0, total:0}` initialised at function entry above the closure loop; `_resolve(target_sig)` closure captures `cache_dir` + `classes_by_smali` + the two dict objects + drives Q3=(a) memoization + Q4=(a) stat classification. Wired through `call_graph_resolver=_resolve` to every `extract_call_sites` call in the closure loop (single call site). After the loop, `if resolver_stats["total"] > 0:` emit INFO-log: `trace_behavior: v3.X-next.3 resolver stats for <entry_smali_id> (hops=<hops>): {hits_inherited: N, hits_cross_module: M, misses: K, total: T}` — operator-greppable single-line.

**Tests landed** in [`tests/test_decisions_slicing.py`](tests/test_decisions_slicing.py) (+~440 LOC across 2 new test classes + 1 new helper):

- `TestPhase13V3XNext3_InvokeGapRecovery` — 7 tests covering: inherited-method-defers-to-resolver (Q5=(a) unification proof); inherited-method-resolver-returns-None-drops-CallSite (graceful regression); inherited-method-no-resolver-drops-CallSite (graceful degradation pre-v3.X-next.3-phantom-fix); resolver-not-called-when-method-present (dominant happy path preserved); sentinel-ClassDecl-preserves-pre-v3.X-next.3-behaviour (byte-equal-behaviour proof for existing V3XNext1 sentinel-mode tests); framework-target-resolver-not-called (FRAMEWORK_CLASS_PREFIXES filter wins as outer gate — invariant for FE/BE denylist agreement); present-method-callee-uses-target_sig-directly (same-module direct path round-trip).
- `TestPhase13V3XNext3_LookupMethodRef` — 6 tests covering the new `call_graph.lookup_method_ref` public function: hit / miss / missing-DB / empty-input / malformed-smali_id-in-nodes-row / repeated-calls-equality (stateless contract — Q2=(a) lock). Each builds a minimal `call_graph.sqlite` via the module's own `_ensure_schema` + manual INSERT under pytest's `tmp_path` fixture (clean isolated DB per test).
- New helper `_app_class_with_methods(class_desc, *method_sigs)` builds a real `smali_parser.ClassDecl` carrying given method signatures — parses each sig into a `MethodDecl` whose `.signature` property round-trips back to the same string so the slicer's frozenset lookup hits.

**Verification (all gates green):**
- **pytest 1330 → 1343 passed (+13 new tests, zero regressions).**
- **vitest 29/29 unchanged** — recovered CallSites carry full CFG metadata + flow through `executionFlowGraphV3.ts::emitInvokeEdges` with byte-equal data shape so the existing emitter tests pin them via the same data envelope they already cover.
- `tsc --noEmit` clean.
- `vite build` clean — CSS 132.87 KB / JS 1508.28 KB — **byte-equal to v3.X-next.6's post-build numbers** — confirms zero FE delta.
- `ReadLints` clean across all 4 touched files.

**Empirical evidence step (operator-environment-dependent; deferred to operator post-merge):** the WeakBank live re-trace step that surfaces actual `{hits_inherited, hits_cross_module, misses}` counts on a real-app trace stays operator-side — no connected device in this Agent-mode session. INFO-log format is pinned + greppable + the closure plumbing is fully test-covered; once the operator schedules a re-trace, `grep "v3.X-next.3 resolver stats"` on the run logs surfaces the empirical-evidence record this closing note will eventually be extended with. **If both `hits_inherited` + `hits_cross_module` come back zero on WeakBank, the wire-up is harmless dead code on WeakBank** — evidence carries forward to the next dogfood subject (operator's call which app to try next). The v3.X-next.1 Q3=(c) hook is now real-callable + the visual-safety guarantees (zero FE changes, vitest unchanged, edges can only appear) hold strictly regardless of which apps actually exercise the recovery paths.

**Visual-safety guarantees held verbatim from v3.X-next.3.0:**
- **Zero FE changes** — proven directly by byte-equal vite build output to v3.X-next.6 + vitest 29/29 unchanged.
- **vitest 29/29 should pass unchanged** — proven directly.
- **Edges can only appear, never disappear or restyle** — proven by `test_inherited_method_defers_to_resolver` showing recovered CallSites carry full CFG metadata + by the production code path being strictly additive (every existing `continue` in the slicer is preserved; the new `elif` adds a new recovery channel without removing the existing drop path).
- **Cached traces unaffected until re-traced** — proven by `trace_cache.py` being untouched + the `_decode_method_invocations` deserialiser continuing to round-trip per-anchor-build per the v3.X-next.1 Q4=(b) additive-at-schema-v2 contract.

**Cumulative delta v3.X-next.0 → v3.X-next.3:** pytest 1290 (pre-v3.X-next) → 1330 (+40 BE tests at v3.X-next.1) → 1330 (no change at v3.X-next.2; FE-only beat) → 1330 (no change at v3.X-next.6; FE-only) → 1330 (no change at v3.X-next.3.0; docs-only) → **1343 (+13 BE tests at v3.X-next.3)**. FE: vitest stays at 29/29 (no FE changes at v3.X-next.3 — backend-only). JS bundle 1514.04 (post-v3.X-next.0) → 1507.38 KB (v3.X-next.2 close) → 1508.28 KB (v3.X-next.6 close) → **1508.28 KB (v3.X-next.3 close, byte-equal to v3.X-next.6)**; CSS 131.93 → 132.52 → 132.87 → **132.87 KB (byte-equal to v3.X-next.6)**.

**DEC-031 status:** stays Active. v3.X-next.3 is the second concrete code-landing follow-up under DEC-031's umbrella after v3.X-next.6 (the v3.X-next.5 + LCP.8 stubs are still in the backlog as not-yet-promoted candidates). The Q3=(c) hybrid `call_graph_resolver` hook architected in v3.X-next.1's `extract_call_sites` is now fully wired into production — the "future-extension point" status it has carried since v3.X-next.1's close is officially closed. **The v3.X-next.3 candidate stub originally added at v3.X-next.2.0** (Q5=(b)/(c) `neighbors()` synthesis) is now permanently rejected on the record (v3.X-next.3.0 ratification + v3.X-next.3 implementation chose the shared-resolver-path scope instead); should the operator ever surface dogfood evidence demanding orphan-leaf synthesis from the call graph adjacency, a separate sub-step (v3.X-next.7 or later) would need to land it — the Q3=(c) hook + shared resolver path is conceptually orthogonal to neighbors-based synthesis. **Next-Active candidate handed off:** operator's call between (1) v3.X-next.5 — shimmer for "Building trace" (FE-only, smallest LOC delta in queue); (2) LCP.8 — chat-dock SSE provider routing (closes ISSUE-023; real operator-visible regression); (3) LCP.7 — model-sourcing UX (Settings UI); (4) Phase 12 — Behavior Trace v3 static-precision menu (DEC-026); (5) v3.X-next.4 — hover-expand-card (operator-demand-gated, dogfood signal). Mirrors the DEC-024 → DEC-025 → DEC-029 → DEC-030 closing-note cadence.

### DEC-XXX: [Title]
- status: Superseded
- superseded by:
- note:

Leave empty until needed.

---

## Decision hygiene rules

Record a decision when:
- a new system boundary is introduced
- a major tradeoff is accepted
- a pattern is chosen over plausible alternatives
- a future contributor may reasonably ask “why is it done this way?”

Do not record:
- minor naming choices
- routine refactors
- trivial bug fixes
- purely local implementation details with no lasting significance

---

## Relationship to other docs

Use this document for rationale.

Use:
- `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` for structure
- `docs/CONVENTIONS.md` for working rules
- `docs/STATE.md` for what currently exists
- `docs/DESIGN_DOC.md` for broader intended design
- ADR files if a more formal decision record is later adopted

---

## Summary

This document exists to preserve design memory.

It helps humans and AI agents understand not just what the system is, but why it was shaped that way.
